skip dotfiles\n        if any(part.startswith('.') for part in rel.parts):\n            continue\n        z.write(file_path, arcname=str(rel))\n\n# Validate zip integrity\nwith zipfile.ZipFile(out_hwpx) as z:\n    bad=z.testzip()\n    if bad:\n        raise SystemExit(f'Zip corrupted at {bad}')\nprint('Wrote', out_hwpx)\nPY","description":"Replace placeholders in XML, remove layout caches in modified paragraphs, and repackage to training_feedback_ready.hwpx","timeout":120000,"run_in_background":false,"dangerouslyDisableSandbox":false}}],"model":"openai/gpt-5.2-20251211","stop_reason":null,"stop_details":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":null,"cache_read_input_tokens":null,"cache_creation":null,"inference_geo":null,"server_tool_use":null,"service_tier":null,"speed":"standard"},"provider":"OpenAI","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e99ef643-816f-429e-bc92-36728e33c512","uuid":"fc480b12-8c60-46c2-a4bd-8cd21ad83879"}
